# LOOM Mascot Character Sheets

**Campaign:** mascot-character-sheets

**Concept:** Turnarounds, expression grids, action poses and group scenes for the four LOOM mascots (Spooly, Flick, Nexo, Prism), for consistent use across the site and marketing.

**Count:** 300 prompts | **Aspect ratios cycled:** 4:5, 3:4, 9:16, 1:1, 16:9

**Reference images:**

- /Users/hideyourkids/Desktop/LOOM PROJECT/public/img/crew/lineup.webp
- /Users/hideyourkids/Desktop/LOOM PROJECT/public/img/crew/spooly.webp
- /Users/hideyourkids/Desktop/LOOM PROJECT/public/img/crew/flick.webp
- /Users/hideyourkids/Desktop/LOOM PROJECT/public/img/crew/nexo.webp
- /Users/hideyourkids/Desktop/LOOM PROJECT/public/img/crew/prism.webp

---

## 1. `mascot-character-sheets-000` — scene 0 — 4:5

Spooly full-body turnaround reference sheet: the chubby magenta yarn-spool character with warm wooden spool ends, three consistent poses front/side/back on one canvas, arms-on-hips neutral stance. soft magenta and cyan rim lighting from two sides, deep shadow fill. straight-on eye-level composition. a few loose glowing thread strands drifting across the frame. LOOM brand palette: deep near-black violet background (#0d0716), signature magenta (#f21c8c) and violet (#7b2fbe) as primary glow colors, cyan (#59e6ff) as secondary accent, warm gold (#ffc740) used sparingly for highlights, clean off-white (#f2f0f7) for any light surfaces. High-end 3D animated feature-film character-art style, original in-house design. Do not reference or imitate any real animation studio, franchise, or trademarked character. Do not depict any real company logos or trademarks. LOOM branding must be clearly present in this scene: work a clean, crisply legible 'LOOM' wordmark (all capital letters, spelled exactly L-O-O-M, simple modern sans-serif) into the background somewhere natural to the scene — as signage, a hanging banner, a woven label, a screen graphic, packaging text, or embossed lettering. The wordmark must render as real, correctly spelled, sharp text, not decorative squiggles or fake glyphs. Every product, bottle, package or storefront shown must be explicitly branded as a LOOM product (e.g. its label reads 'LOOM' plus a short product line name), never a generic or unnamed item. Attach lineup.webp plus the individual mascot file most relevant to the scene (e.g. spooly.webp for Spooly-only scenes; use lineup.webp for group scenes). Characters must match the attached reference photos exactly — same designs, colors, proportions, faces. Do not invent different designs.
